Model before rewriting:

find x: int(2..4)
find y: int(2..4)
find z: int(2..4)

such that

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#rank_1: bool
  find z#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#rank_1: bool
  find x#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#rank_1: bool
  find y#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#rank_2),and([!(y#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15,
or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
or([!(x#rank_2),and([!(x#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
or([!(y#rank_2),and([!(y#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

or([!(y#rank_2),and([!(y#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

  assert(or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin
